08

Integrated Forecast

Sidebar › Integrated ForecastAccess: Integrated Forecast

Collects VINFAST · KGM · Hyundai · Kia · CKD · GLOBAL · manual entries by month, to show the forecast revenue of the whole company.

Screen layout · top to bottom
  1. Title barExport (current tab as CSV)
  2. Condition barBase Month · Unit Amount/Qty (Overview only) · Data Status chips (VINFAST · KGM · HKMC · CKD)
  3. TabsOverview CKD · GLOBAL Hyundai · Kia KGM VINFAST Manual Entry
  4. BodyCards · charts · tables of the selected tab

See this month's outlook

Step by step · View the Overview
  1. Choose a month in Base Month.

    At first, the latest month with data is selected. Future months show · Forecast.

  2. Check the Data Status chips.

    Teal = that customer's data is uploaded; gray = not yet. If a chip is gray, that customer's amount is missing.

  3. Look at the cards in the Overview tab.

    Total Forecast · vs. Last Month · Top Customer · Customers Loaded, then region cards below (VINFAST / Domestic / Export) and charts.

  4. In the Detail by customer table, click View.

    It moves to that customer's tab, where you can view by Model · Item Name · part number.

MarkMeaning
▲ ▼ NewUp vs. last month · down · no value last month
ManualAmount entered by hand in the Manual Entry tab
Shipment basisVINFAST past months = actual shipped amount. This month and later = S/L adjusted forecast
(Not loaded)No data uploaded for that month

In a customer tab, narrow down with view buttons such as Summary · Model · Item Name · Detail and the search box. VINFAST › Detail is a table of unit price · Vietnam/India quantity · amount by part number. Below it, the screen tells you whether it matches the summary total (Same as summary total / Difference N KRW).

Add manual revenue (forklift · TATA · bus, etc.)

Step by step · Manual Entry
  1. Choose a month in Base Month and click the Manual Entry tab.

    A Forklift / TATA / Bus row (amount 0) is already there.

  2. Enter Item Name · Amount (KRW) · Note.

    To add a row, click Add item. To delete one, click the trash can at the end of the row.

  3. Click Save.

    When Saved appears, the Overview is recalculated.

Each month is saved separately. Rows with an empty item name are not saved.

Where do the numbers come from?

Next · VF Dashboard →
09

VF Dashboard

Sidebar › Customers › VINFAST › top menu DashboardAccess: VF Dashboard

See one month of VINFAST at a glance. S/L (target) · production plan · production actuals · shipments are placed side by side for the same month.

Screen layout · top to bottom
  1. Title barUnit: 100M (EA for quantity) · Refresh Data
  2. Filter barItem · Model · Year · Month · Unit Amount/Qty
  3. 4 number cardsForecast (S/L) · Production plan · Production actuals · Shipment basis (all for the selected month)
  4. ChartsMonthly comparison (Jan–Dec) · Month N daily cumulative · Comparison by item · Contribution by model
  5. TableItem Summary
Step by step · Check this month's progress
  1. Choose Year · Month.

    The 4 cards and the daily cumulative chart change to that month.

  2. If needed, narrow down with Item · Model.

    Example: item DAB · model VF8. The charts and table below change together.

  3. Switch between Amount / Qty.

    Amount is in units of 100 million KRW; quantity is in EA.

  4. Use the Month N daily cumulative chart to see progress against the target.

    Use the S/L · Production plan · Production actuals · Shipment buttons above the chart to turn lines off and on. Click the zoom icon at the top right of the chart to see it larger.

How to read the charts

  • S/L is the target for the month, so it is drawn as a flat line near the top (Vietnam and India separately).
  • Production actuals · Shipments are cumulative totals added day by day from the 1st, and are drawn only up to today. The production plan is drawn to the end of the month.
  • Hover the mouse over a point to see the cumulative value up to that day and the value for that day.
  • For the Contribution by model donut, use the basis buttons above it to choose S/L · production plan · production actuals · shipments.
  • Amounts use the confirmed FX rate of that month (VF Revenue).
If the yellow bar Some part numbers have no registered unit price. appears below, those part numbers are left out of the amounts. Add them in Upload › Price Management. If you cannot add them right away, use the clock button on the bar to choose an option such as Don't show for a day.

Refresh Data downloads everything again from the server, so it takes time. Normally you do not need to click it; the data updates by itself.

Next · VF Revenue (FX rate · Adjustment ratio) →
10

VF Revenue (FX rate · Adjustment ratio)

VINFAST top menu › VF RevenueAccess: VF Revenue

Calculates the forecast revenue for each month as S/L quantity × unit price × FX rate. You set and save the FX rate and adjustment ratios here. The saved FX rate is also used for amounts in the Dashboard · Integrated Forecast · Shipment Status.

Screen layout (title: Revenue Forecast (S/L Basis))
  1. Top buttonsYear · Vietnam India Combined · Export
  2. Formula lineAdjusted forecast = forecast × global ratio × monthly ratio
  3. Input fieldsFX Rate (USD→KRW) · Adjustment Ratio (Global) · Save Settings to Sheet
  4. Monthly fieldsAdjustment Ratio (Monthly) Jan–Dec · Monthly FX rate applied (USD→₩) Jan–Dec · Recheck
  5. Tables① Forecast (adjustment ratio applied) · ② Forecast (₩) · ③ S/L plan (adjustment ratio applied) · ④ S/L plan
Step by step · Save the FX rate and adjustment ratios
  1. Click Vietnam or India and choose a year.

    In Combined, the input fields are locked (because it is not clear which region the values belong to).

  2. Enter the FX rate · global adjustment ratio · monthly adjustment ratios.

    The tables below are recalculated as soon as you type. You can see the result before saving.

  3. Click Save Settings to Sheet.

    When Saved successfully appears, you are done. The Integrated Forecast is also recalculated with the new FX rate.

FX rateHow it is used
Past monthsConfirmed and locked at the average rate of that month (confirmed automatically on the 1st of each month). Changing the rate above does not change past months' amounts.
This month · futureUses the value in FX Rate (USD→KRW) above.
Correcting a confirmed valueIf it differs from the bank's published rate, edit that month's field in Monthly FX rate applied directly. It is saved as soon as you leave the field. Months that have not passed yet cannot be edited.

Example: forecast 10 million KRW × global 80% × monthly 100% = adjusted forecast 8 million KRW. Click a model row (VF6 · VF8, etc.) in the table to expand the items below it. Export saves the data as filtered, and expands any collapsed models in the file.

Next · VF S/L (Monthly · Rolling · Production request) →
11

VF S/L (Monthly · Rolling · Production request)

VINFAST top menu › VF S/L ▾Access: Monthly S/L · Rolling S/L · Production Request

Shows when and how much the customer asked for (S/L). Each time a weekly S/L is uploaded, that day's order is kept as one snapshot (a round, example: 07-21_SL).

Monthly S/L — one month across many rounds

Choose a month and see across the screen how that month's order changed in each round. The further right, the newer, and up to 12 rounds are shown.

Step by step · Monthly S/L
  1. Choose one of Vietnam · India · Combined, then choose the year · month.
  2. In Value, choose Qty / Amount.

    For amounts, the Applied FX rate is shown too.

  3. Filter by part number · item · model · part name.

    When you filter, the total of only the selected rows appears below in a teal row.

In Combined view, Vietnam and India snapshots with close dates are grouped into one round and shown like August round 1. If one side has no snapshot for that round, its previous value is carried over.

Rolling S/L — many months from one round

Step by step · Rolling S/L
  1. Choose the region (Vietnam/India/Combined) and the round.

    At first, the latest round is selected.

  2. Look at the Item summary table at the top and the part-number table below.

    Columns go in the order Back Order (overdue quantity) → M-2 … M+8. The larger the Back Order, the redder the cell.

Production request — spreading the backorder over months

This is not a screen for entering a number for each part number. It is a screen for setting what % of the overdue quantity (backorder) to produce in which month.

Production request = that month's S/L + backorder × that month's ratio (rounded up). Vietnam and India are calculated together.
Step by step · Set production request ratios
  1. Choose a round.

    At first, the last round of the previous month is selected. The latest round is marked ★ Latest.

  2. Enter % in the Backorder split ratio fields (current month to M+8).

    The table is recalculated right away. If the total is less than 100%, the rest goes to the last month.

  3. Click Save.

    When the button turns green, it is saved (red = failed). Everyone sees the same ratios. To go back to the start, click Reset.

  4. Check the table and download it with Export.

    In View:, you can narrow down with Backorder only · Request qty only.

Next · VF Production plan · Plan vs Actual · Production actuals →
12

VF Production plan · Plan vs Actual · Production actuals

VINFAST top menu › VF Plan ▾ · VF ProductionAccess: Production plan · Plan vs Actual

Shows when, what and how much was planned (plan), and how much was actually made (actuals).

Production plan · Production actuals

Step by step · View plan / actuals
  1. Choose Year · Month.

    To set a date range yourself, enter a start date ~ end date in Period:.

  2. In View By, choose Daily / Weekly / Monthly.
  3. Choose Qty / Amount and filter by part number · part name · item · model.

    The cards at the top show Part Numbers · Total Planned Qty (EA) (for actuals, Total actual qty) · Period.

  • Production actuals count good parts only (defects are excluded).
  • Amount = quantity × unit price (USD) × that month's FX rate. Part numbers with no unit price show 0.
  • The production plan flows into Stock Status as Planned Prod. and increases available stock.

Plan vs Actual

Step by step · View the achievement rate
  1. Choose the Year and the start week ~ end week.

    Move one week at a time with ◀ ▶, and return with This week. A week is Monday to Saturday.

  2. Choose Daily / Weekly / Monthly. If needed, filter by Line: and part number, etc.

    To clear all filters, click Reset filters.

  3. Check the Achievement on the cards and the differences in the table.
Achievement = actuals ÷ planColor
100% or moreGreen Produced at or above plan
90% or moreYellow Slightly short
Under 90%Red Falling behind
Next · VF Shipment Status · Production vs Shipment →
13

VF Shipment Status · Production vs Shipment

VINFAST top menu › VF Shipment ▾Access: Shipment Status · Production vs Shipment

Shows the quantities and amounts actually shipped. Used for monthly closing and performance reports.

Shipment Status

Step by step · View shipment actuals
  1. Choose Vietnam / India / Combined.
  2. Choose Year · Month.

    At first, the latest month with shipments is selected.

  3. Set Date Basis: to Registered date or Shipped date.
  4. Choose View By · Value (quantity/amount) and filter by part number, etc.
  • FX: cannot be edited. It is the value saved in VF Revenue. If the label next to it is Sales Detail · VN x · IN y, it is normal. If it is Default, save the FX rate once in VF Revenue.
  • Part numbers with no unit price are counted in quantity, but their amount is 0.

Production vs Shipment

Step by step · Compare what was made with what was shipped
  1. Choose the region (Combined at first) and the start month ~ end month.

    Return straight to the current month with This month.

  2. Choose Weekly / Monthly and Line.
  3. Narrow down with View:.

    All Parts · With Difference · Not Shipped Only · Over-shipped Only

Shipment rate = shipments ÷ production actuals. Quantity made but not yet shipped = actuals − shipments. Use this to find part numbers piling up in the warehouse. If you choose India, the actuals column changes to S/L Plan.

Next · Available Stock (upload invoices) →
14

Available Stock (upload invoices)

VINFAST top menu › VF Stock › Available StockAccess: Available Stock

Shows the quantity you can really ship now: warehouse stock minus the quantity already promised on invoices. It prevents you from promising the same goods twice.

Screen layout · top to bottom
  1. Top buttonsInvoice Folder (jumps to the folder below) · Show help · Export
  2. Settings barTransport Sea/Air · S/L All/Vietnam/India · Period Current/Next month · Backorder
  3. 4 cardsMonthly S/L · S/L Remaining This Month · Invoice · Shippable (invoice applied)
  4. Part-number table… Available · Invoice · Invoice No. · Shippable · S/L Remaining
  5. Invoice folderPending INV Shipped Previous Rev · Add Invoice · drop area · region · transport · search

Upload invoice Excel files

Invoices issued in INV Issue do not need to be uploaded. The moment they are saved, they enter Pending INV and are deducted. The steps below are for adding invoice Excel files received from elsewhere.
Step by step · Add invoices
  1. Go to the Invoice Folder below.

    Click the Invoice Folder button at the top to jump straight down.

  2. Drag and drop Excel files (.xlsx · .xls) or click Add Invoice.

    You can add several files at once. A progress bar and the status of each file appear (green = done, red = blocked).

  3. Check the result.

    For files that could not be added, the reason is shown. If the file name has no AIR · SEA, the transport is set to Unspecified.

When you upload a corrected version of the same invoice (Rev)

  • Add a Rev number to the end of the file name: VF260813-250_SEA.xlsx → VF260813-250_SEA_Rev01.xlsx → …_Rev02.xlsx
  • If it is a higher Rev, the old version moves to Previous Rev and the new one is deducted. A same or lower Rev is not added, and the reason is shown.
  • Formats such as REV.3 · _R02 are also recognized. You can open older versions any time in Previous Rev.

Choose the conditions for the shippable calculation

Step by step · Set the conditions
  1. Choose Transport.
    • Sea: choose Days (next N days) or Period (start date ~ end date). Available stock = actual stock + production plan up to then. Because of packing time, the plan for the last 2 days before the end date is not counted.
    • Air: production actuals from the last N days (1–5 days) are treated as not yet packed and are subtracted.
  2. In S/L, choose All / Vietnam / India.
  3. Set Period to Current or Next month.

    Next month shows in advance how much you can ship next month (the Shipped column changes to Planned Prod., S/L Remaining changes to Achievement rate, and the transport settings are locked).

  4. To include the overdue quantity, turn on Backorder.
MarkMeaning
ShippableAvailable stock − pending invoices. Shipped invoices and Previous Rev are not subtracted.
S/L Remaining number (red background)The part of this month's target not yet filled. The larger it is, the darker the red
+N · DoneAbove the target · exactly on target
Red ! before a part numberMore invoices were issued than the available stock
Available stock colorRed = negative · gray = 0 · the darker the orange, the more stock
  • This month's target (current-month S/L) is fixed by the last S/L round of the previous month, so it does not change in the middle of the month. The backorder uses the latest round.
  • For part numbers used by both Vietnam and India, India's share is filled first, and the rest is counted as Vietnam's shippable quantity.

Export a CSV for INV auto-issue

Step by step · Make the CSV
  1. In S/L, choose Vietnam or India.
  2. Set the transport · dates · period.
  3. In View: above the table, choose Shippable.
  4. Click Export.

    인보이스관리_날짜_해상_지역.csv (Invoice management_date_sea_region) is downloaded. Upload this file to INV Issue › CSV auto-issue.

In the Shipped tab, Clear completed list cannot be undone.

15

Stock Status

VINFAST top menu › VF Stock › Stock StatusAccess: VF Stock

Shows by part number what is in the warehouse now, how much, and how much you can ship from now on.

Step by step · View stock
  1. In S/L, choose All / Vietnam / India.
  2. Choose Transport.

    Sea = adds Production plan for the next [7] days · Air = Subtract packing for the last [3] days. You can change the number in the field.

  3. Check the cards and warning lines at the top.

    Actual Stock · Planned Production (Air: packing pending) · Remaining This Month · Shippable. Click View on the shortage · backorder · dead stock line to show only those part numbers.

  4. Narrow down with View and choose Sort.

    All Shippable Shortage Backorder Dead stock Negative Zero stock

How to read the table (left → right)

ColumnMeaning
CountQuantity counted in the latest stock count — the starting point
＋ActualsGood parts made after the stock count
−ShipmentsQuantity shipped after the stock count
＝Actual stockQuantity in the warehouse now
Planned Prod. / Packing pendingSea adds what will be made; Air subtracts what is not yet packed
AvailableQuantity you can really ship

Shippable (after invoices) and S/L Remaining are shown on the Available Stock screen. Upload stock count data in Upload › Stock Count.

Next · HMC · KMC · KGM · CKD screens →
16

HMC · KMC · KGM · CKD screens

Sidebar › Customers › HMC · KMC · KGM · CKDAccess: Customers

Shows the data of one customer only. The selected customer's name is added before the top menu names (for CKD, CKD · GLOBAL).

  • DashboardMonthly · Yearly · FY
  • S/L ▾S/L · Production request
  • Production plan ▾Production plan · Plan vs Actual
  • Shipment Status ▾Shipment Status · Production vs Shipment
  • Shipment ManagementPart number × period table
  • Stock Status ▾Stock Status

When you change the customer, the tab you were viewing stays the same.

Dashboard · S/L

  • Dashboard: Same layout as the VF Dashboard. Period has Monthly · Yearly · FY (April to March of the next year). Month is used only in Monthly.
  • S/L: A table of part numbers (rows) × months (columns), with subtotals on the right and totals at the bottom. Filter with Qty/Amount and item · model · part number · part name. Clear the filters with the eraser icon.
  • Amounts: HMC · KMC · KGM use the integrated unit price (KRW); CKD uses the CKD unit price. CKD dollar prices are converted to KRW at that month's FX rate.

Enter a production request

Enter the request quantity for each part number directly in the request quantity column on the right of the S/L table.

Step by step · Customer production request
  1. Choose the Request month.

    At first it is this month. You can also choose a later month to enter in advance.

  2. (Optional) Click Apply all (S/L qty) → Apply.

    This only fills every field with the S/L quantity. It is not saved yet.

  3. To save one part number: enter the number and press Enter → Change.

    It asks Change to this value? and saves only that part number right away.

  4. To save many part numbers at once: enter the numbers, then click Save all → Save.
  5. Check the mark in each field.

    Not saved · Saving · ✓ Saved · Failed

Production plan · Shipments · Shipment Management · Stock Status

  • Production plan · Plan vs Actual · Shipment Status · Production vs Shipment: Daily/Weekly/Monthly, Qty/Amount, year · month, item · model · part number · part name. Achievement and shipment rates are green at 100% or more and red under 80%.
  • Shipment Management: A part number × period table. At first it shows this month. Set Date basis to Registered date/Shipped date.
  • Stock Status: Actual stock = stock count + production after the count − shipments (with no stock count, counted from January 1 of this year). Available stock = actual stock + Production plan for the next [7] days (0–60 days, remembered on this PC). Negative actual stock is red. Narrow down with Hide zero stock, and upload stock count files with Upload Stock Count.
  • Available Stock is not ready yet on the customer screens.
Forklift / TATA / Bus has no screen data yet, so clicking it shows the Coming soon window. Enter revenue for these customers in Integrated Forecast › Manual Entry.
Next · INV Issue →
17

INV Issue

Sidebar › Operations › INV IssueAccess: INV Issue

Here you create invoices, download INV · PL · PLDETAIL · TEMP documents, and correct (Rev) or merge invoices. The moment you save, the invoice is deducted from Available Stock and added as a card to VF Shipment Management.

Screen layout · top to bottom
  1. Top horizontal menuIssue Invoice · INV EDIT · Dashboard Analysis · Issue History (Log) · Upload Past Records · Master Settings (☰ on a phone)
  2. General InfoShipping Category · Consignee Address (Consignee) · Transport · Issue Date (Date) · Invoice No. · on the right, Drag or click a CSV file · Auto-issue
  3. Item and box quantity input areaPallet count (Total N / 80 pallets) · 80-pallet switch · Add item row · item rows and 6 preview fields
  4. BottomLeft: Reset · Right: Search & Download · Save & Create

Issue one invoice by hand

Step by step · Issue an invoice
  1. Choose the Shipping Category.

    AKC · AKC_India · KMS · DH AUTO. Changing it clears the items you entered, so choose it first. If you choose AKC_India, the address changes to India automatically; if you choose AKC, it changes to Vietnam.

  2. Check Consignee Address · Transport · Issue Date.

    Transport is Sea (SEA) · Air (AIR). The issue date is today by default. The Invoice No. is filled in automatically.

  3. Enter the part number.

    Choose it with Search or type it into the field. Part name · SA number · unit price · quantity per box are filled in automatically. If it is another company's part number, the Category mismatch window appears and the field is cleared.

  4. Enter Box Qty (Cartons) or Total Qty (EA) and leave the field.

    When you enter one, the other is adjusted, and the value is rounded down to full pallets. The preview below shows total quantity · unit price · amount · pallets · net weight · gross weight.

  5. If there are more items, click Add item row.

    ⊕ at the top right of a row adds a row below it; ⊖ deletes that row.

  6. Click Save & Create.

    Issued appears. Next to Document number confirmed: …, the buttons INV PL PLDETAIL TEMP ALL IN ONE ✉ Mail appear.

  7. Choose an option in the Send the email? window.

    To send now, go to the mail window (→ Send INV email). To send later, click Don't send.

Number prefixVietnamIndia
AKCVF—
AKC_India—VFIN
KMSVNSTWINSTW
DH AUTOVNDHINDH

Number = prefix + issue date (YYMMDD) - serial number. Example: VF260905-156. A corrected version gets _Rev01 · _Rev02 at the end.

Turn the 80-pallet limit on and off

It is the switch to the right of the pallet count, next to the title of the item input area. When you refresh the page, it goes back to on (ON).

On (ON)Off (OFF)
Manual entryThe moment it goes over 80, that entry is cancelled and the Pallet limit exceeded window appearsEntered as is, even over 80
CSV auto-issueSplit into several invoices of 80 pallets eachIssued as one invoice, not split
Pallet countYellow from 72 · red over 80Only Total N pallets

CSV auto-issue

Step by step · Issue many invoices at once with a CSV
  1. Download the CSV from Available Stock.

    → Available Stock › Export CSV

  2. Drag and drop that file onto the Drag or click a CSV file · Auto-issue box.

    AKC Vietnam / AKC India is set from the region in the file, and the issue date is the file's creation date. Transport is saved as SEA.

  3. Check the CSV auto-issue result window.

    Invoices are created for the maximum quantity that can be shipped in full pallets. Part numbers left out are listed in Excluded part numbers with the reason (no order · no pallet spec · no unit price · less than 1 pallet).

If saving fails partway, even the invoices already saved are all cancelled, and Auto-issue failed (rolled back) appears. Just upload the same file again.

Download documents

Step by step · Get documents
  1. Click one of INV · PL · PLDETAIL · TEMP · ALL IN ONE.

    For invoices issued earlier, use the buttons on each row of the Search & Download list.

  2. In Select format, choose Download PDF file or Download Excel file.

    After a progress window, the file is saved with the name number_transport_Rev_document.

ButtonDocument
INVCommercial Invoice
PLPacking list summary
PLDETAILDetailed packing by pallet
TEMPTemplate for VF upload (text only, no formatting)
ALL IN ONEThe four above in one file (4 tabs)

On documents with an issue date of 2026-10-05 or later, the VinFast company name is printed with the new name (Future Production, Investment and Development Joint Stock Company). Invoices before that keep the old name, even if downloaded again.

Correct an invoice (Rev)

The original stays as it is, and the correction is saved as a new version with _Rev01 added. There are two ways.

Step by step · Correct items · quantities
  1. Click Search & Download.

    The History & Revisions window opens. Search by number · date · company · part number.

  2. Click Edit on the row to correct.

    The yellow bar Revision (edit) mode appears and the content is loaded into the issue screen. Check again that the consignee address and transport are correct.

  3. Correct the content and click Save & Create.

    To stop, click Cancel edit (reset).

Step by step · Remove / add pallets (INV EDIT)
  1. Click INV EDIT in the top menu → Edit in the list.
  2. Choose a mode.

    Delete items (pallet control): turn off the include switch of the pallets to remove. Add/edit items (form editor): change items (has the 80-pallet switch).

  3. Check the preview on the right and click …Confirm and issue new revision (Rev).

    Transport (AIR/SEA) is taken over from the original invoice.

Merge · Delete

Step by step · Merge several invoices into one
  1. In the History & Revisions window, check 2 or more invoices to merge.

    Clicking anywhere on a row also checks it. You can merge only invoices of the same shipping category.

  2. Click the yellow Merge selected (n).
  3. Choose the base invoice with the round button.

    The base shows Base (keep), and the others show Marked as merged after merging.

  4. Check the new number (expected) · number of items · total quantity · amount, and click Run merge.

    The result is one new Rev of the base invoice. Rows with the same part number and the same unit price are combined into one row. The issue date and transport of the base invoice are used.

  • Merged invoices are not deleted. They get a Merged mark and are removed from Available Stock and Shipment Management.
  • The merged result is not subject to the 80-pallet limit.
  • Delete: check the invoices → Delete selected (n) → confirm. At first, only the last Rev of each number is shown. To see all, click Show all history; to go back, click Show final versions.
Deletion cannot be undone. The invoice is removed completely from the issue history · totals · numbering, and its card in Shipment Management also disappears. Reset and changing the category also clear any content that is not saved.

Where does an issued invoice go?

What you did in INV IssueAvailable StockVF Shipment Management
New issueEnters Pending INV and is deductedA waiting card is created
Correction (Rev)The new Rev is deducted; the old version goes to Previous RevNew Rev card · the old version becomes a yellow Previous Rev card
MergeOnly the new merged Rev is deductedOnly the merged card remains
DeleteThe deduction is released and the stock comes backThe card disappears

The logistics team does not need to click anything. The Shipment Management screen changes by itself and an alert sounds.

Next · Send INV email · Send log →
17.1

Send INV email · Send log

INV Issue › right after saving · ✉ Mail · ✉ Send in the listAccess: INV Issue (your account needs a company email address)

Send the issued invoice documents to the customer by email. The email goes out from the company sending account, and you are added to Reply-To and CC.

Mail window · left side, top to bottom (preview on the right)
  1. InvoiceNumber · region · transport · issue date · sender · one line of send log and View log →
  2. My signatureEnglish name · title · department (saved for each account)
  3. To · CCCollapsed at first · Expand to edit ▼
  4. Subject · RemarksSubject in English only · if you write remarks in Korean, the AI changes them to English
  5. AttachmentsCheck documents · format Excel/PDF · 📎 Add file
  6. Bottom⚙ Default settings · Don't send · ✉ Send
Step by step · Send an invoice email
  1. Open the mail window.

    Use the Send the email? window right after saving, ✉ Mail on the issue screen, or, in the History & Revisions list, ✉ Send. If you check several invoices, send them together with ✉ Send selected invoices.

  2. The first time, set up My signature.

    ✎ Edit → English name · title · department → 💾 Save my signature.

  3. Check To · CC with Expand to edit ▼.

    Type an address and press Enter (or paste) to add it; click × to remove it. Drag an address or use ⇄ to move it between To ↔ CC. Add me to CC (copy to me) is on by default.

  4. Write the remarks in the Korean field.

    The AI changes them into the English review field. When ✓ Translated appears, read the English and correct it. To translate again, click Translate again.

  5. Choose the attachments.

    Check ALL IN ONE / INV / PL / DETAIL / TEMP, and choose Format Excel/PDF. Add other files with 📎 Add file (10 MB per file · up to 10 files). For a Rev, also check Changes and Append previous email.

  6. Check the preview on the right and click ✉ Send.

    Email sending completed appears. The actual result arrives as a notice at the bottom right of the screen.

View the send log

Click View log → in the mail window to see the send log of the first issue and all Revs, newest first. Open the body that was sent at that time with View body. To go back, click ← Back to compose.

✉ button in the listMeaning
✉ Send (red border)Never sent yet
✉ Sent NSent (N times)
✉ Sent N ⚠Some addresses did not receive it — check the rejected addresses in the log
✉ FailedThe last try failed
Sent means the company mail server accepted it. It does not guarantee that the customer received it. If the result is unclear, check the send log before sending again. Extra attached files are not kept in the system, so keep the originals yourself.

In ⚙ Default settings, set the To · CC · greeting name · mail template · default attachments for each region. Changes apply to everyone, so check with Test mail to me before clicking 💾 Save defaults.

Next · VF Shipment Management →
18

VF Shipment Management

Sidebar › Operations › VF Shipment ManagementAccess: VF Shipment Management

Handles on site everything until an invoice actually goes out (Pickup Request → shipment). It is made for use on a phone, and it tells you about changes with sound and voice.

  • ① IssueINV Issue · Excel upload → waiting card
  • ② Pickup RequestEnter pickup date · truck (a promise)
  • ③ ShipRecord the day it actually left
  • ④ DoneRegistered in receivables automatically
Screen layout · top to bottom
  1. Title · buttons on the right🔔 Alerts on · 📲 Phone alerts · 🔊 Sound test · Download
  2. Tool barView Waiting / Pickup Request / Done / Cancelled / Previous Rev · Region · Transport · search (invoice number · part number)
  3. 4 cardsInvoices · Pallets · Pickup Request (sky blue) · Blocked (red)
  4. Invoice cardsWaiting: Pickup requests · handle first at the very top, then newest issue date first · Done/Cancelled/Previous Rev: collapsed by month
Card lookMeaning
Sky blueA pickup request is set — 📅 pickup date · note (truck · driver) are shown
RedHas blocked pallets — the invoice must be corrected and a new Rev issued (shown before sky blue cards)
Yellow left barPrevious Rev — view only
GrayShipped or cancelled invoice
No packing listThe Excel file has no PACKING DETAILS — delete it from Available Stock and upload it again

View details

Click Detail on a card to see date · region · transport · number of part numbers · EA · boxes · weight, and three tabs.

  • Packing details: Same layout as PACKING DETAILS in the Excel file (the tab that opens first). Use it to check against the Excel file line by line.
  • By part · By pallet: Short tables of what was loaded, how much, and on which pallet.
  • At the bottom: Download Excel (current tab) · Ship · Close.

Make a pickup request

Step by step · Pickup Request
  1. Click Pickup Request on the card.
  2. Choose the Pickup date and write the truck · driver · time in Note.

    Example: 12A-3456 · Driver Kim 010-0000-0000 · 09:00 (up to 80 characters)

  3. Click Save request.

    The card turns sky blue and moves to the top group of Waiting. It appears right away on other people's screens too.

  4. If anything changes, correct it with Edit request.

    To remove the request, click Cancel request at the top right of the window.

A pickup request is a promise; Ship is the record that it actually left. Even after a request, the deduction from Available Stock stays the same, so click Ship after the goods actually leave. To leave only a note without a pickup request, use Memo on the card.

Mark pallets as blocked

When logistics reports "this pallet cannot be loaded" (no stock · damage · label error, etc.), mark that pallet. Based on this mark, the invoice is corrected and a new Rev is issued.

Step by step · Request block
  1. In Detail › Packing details, click the row of the pallet.

    Click anywhere on the row to select it. The bar at the bottom of the screen shows N pallets selected.

  2. Click Request block on the bottom bar.
  3. Write the Reason and click Mark as blocked.

    The row turns red and the card shows Blocked: N pallets. Hover the mouse over it to see the reason · who · when.

  4. Correct that invoice in INV Issue and issue a new Rev.

    When the new Rev comes in, the old version moves to Previous Rev.

  • To remove the mark, keep the row checked and click Clear mark.
  • If only some pallets are blocked, shipping is not blocked (the mark is for notice). The original packing list does not change.
  • When no pallet can be shipped: Cancel all on the bottom bar → reason → the card changes to a red Cancel invoice → Cancel invoice → check the Cancel date → Process cancellation. The invoice moves to the Cancel view.

Ship · Undo

Step by step · Process the shipment
  1. After the goods actually leave, click Ship on the card.
  2. Check the Ship date.

    Today is the default.

  3. Click Ship.

    The card moves to Done, the deduction from shippable stock is released, and the invoice is registered in VF Receivables automatically.

If you clicked by mistake, go to that card in the Done view and click Back to pickup request → Undo. The ship date is cleared.

Find by part number · Progress sheet

  • Find by part number: Enter 4 or more characters of a part number in the search box. A table of invoices containing that part number (status · pallets · PL number range · boxes · quantity) appears above the list. Click a number to open the details.
  • Progress sheet: Click Download to get a progress table by invoice (출하진행표_[지역]_[운송]_날짜.xls, shipment progress_[region]_[transport]_date). It follows the region · transport · search conditions, and puts Waiting · Done · Cancelled on one sheet (only Previous Rev is left out). Row colors: green = shipped, gray = cancelled, light blue = in progress.
Progress sheet columnDate entered
Issued to VFIssue date in the number (VF260813-249 → 8/13)
Forwarder Contact ConfirmedDay the pickup request was first entered
Pickup Volume Cross CheckOne day before the truck booking was confirmed
Pickup date FixedTruck booking confirmation date (in brackets = planned pickup date)
Pickup CompletedDate entered in Ship

If a date is empty in the progress sheet, click Edit request for that invoice in Shipment Management and save it once.

Alert sounds · Phone alerts

When someone issues · makes a pickup request · blocks · ships · cancels, the screen changes right away and an alert comes (your own actions do not sound).

ButtonWhat it does
🔔 Alerts on / 🔕 Alerts offTurns sound · voice messages on this device on and off (example: Invoice 274 has been shipped). It is on at first. Click OK in the alert window to stop it.
🔊 Sound testTests whether sound plays.
📲 Phone alertsShows alerts in the phone's notification list even when the app is in the background.
Step by step · Turn on phone alerts
  1. On your phone (app or Chrome), open VF Shipment Management and tap 📲 Phone alerts.
  2. In the permission window, tap Allow.

    The button changes to 📲 Receiving alerts (📲 App alerts (voice) in the installed app).

  3. Tap it again and check with 🔔 Send test alert.

    Tap ⏱ Test in 10 seconds and press the Home button to put the app in the background. You can then see whether alerts arrive in the background too. If there is a problem, use 🩺 Check alerts on this device. To turn them off, use 🔕 Turn off alerts on this device.

Phone alert messageWhen
Invoice 274 has been issuedNew issue in INV Issue
Invoice 274 has been changedRev · quantity · note, etc. changed
Pickup requested for invoice 274Pickup request · pickup date set
Blocked items requested for invoice 274Blocked mark
Invoice 274 has been shippedShip
Invoice 274 has been cancelledDelete · cancel all
  • No sound: Browsers block sound until you tap the screen once. Tap anywhere on the screen once. If the PC asks to allow notifications, allow them.
  • If 📲 Alerts blocked appears, go to phone Settings → Apps → AKC (or Chrome) → Notifications → Allow.
  • On site phones, the screen does not turn off while Shipment Management is open, and a hard pull-down does not refresh it. Still, the surest way is to set a long screen timeout on the phone.

VF Receivables

Sidebar › Operations › VF ReceivablesAccess: VF Receivables (editing: Master · Admin · users with access to this screen)

Manages how much of the export invoice payments has come in and how much is left. When you click Ship, the invoice is registered here automatically.

Screen layout · top to bottom
  1. Top menu barReceivables Status · Closing Analysis
  2. Right of the titleToday's rate · Closing rate · Auto Email · Download
  3. 4 cardsAll · Paid · Unpaid · Unpaid balance (KRW, today) — click one of the first three to show the list
  4. Filter barSearch: · Status: All/Unpaid/Overdue/Done · Period: Due/Export declaration date · sort by Export declaration date · Detail columns · New
  5. TableInvoice number · export declaration date · currency · billed · paid · balance · FX gain/loss · Due · status
MarkMeaning
Done · Unpaid · OverdueStatus (Overdue blinks)
Due column D+nn days past the due date (blinks red)
Due column within D-3 / within D-7Blinks red / blinks yellow

Record a payment (split payments are OK)

Step by step · Record a payment
  1. Click the invoice row in the table.

    The Payment record · edit — No. area opens above the table.

  2. Click Add payment and enter the payment date · foreign currency amount (USD) · FX rate.

    If the payment came in two or more parts, add more rounds. If you leave the FX rate empty, that day's rate is used.

  3. Check the Payment progress bar.

    Paid X / Billed Y and % are shown. At 100%, · Done is added.

  4. Click Save.

    To delete a round, click ⊖ at the end of the row. Use Details to see the payment history together with the shipped invoice items.

Many items at once · Other buttons

  • Check the left side of rows to show a dark bar: Mark paid (full amount at today's rate) · Mark unpaid (clears payment records) · Delete · Clear selection.
  • New: add an invoice by hand (invoice number · customer · export declaration date · Due date · carried over from last month · receivable (increase) · payment).
  • Closing rate: for quarter-end valuation (June · September · December · March). Enter the Closing date and the USD · JPY · EUR · INR rates and click Save. The period-end valuation and valuation gain/loss of all items are recalculated.
  • Detail columns: expands and collapses the carried-over and period-end valuation columns. Download: Excel exactly as filtered (formatting kept · colors excluded).
Delete also deletes the payment history and cannot be undone. If a shipment is undone or cancelled, the automatically registered item is removed, but items with recorded payments remain.

Closing Analysis

Upload export billing · refund data and check it against receivables, invoice by invoice. Receivable · payment data is not changed.

Step by step · Review closing data
  1. Click Closing Analysis in the top menu → check the Analysis month → Upload data.
  2. Drag and drop files, or click Add file · Select folder.

    Export billing PDF (1 at a time) · refund fee XLSX · refund ledger · payment decision notice · refund application. Up to 12 files · 12 MB per file. The review starts as soon as you choose the files.

  3. Read the result.

    Cards (rows read · matched · not matched · to check further) → Data check items → Invoice matching table (View evidence) → Refunds · Fees table. With the Result selector, you can show only Items to check.

  4. Download the result with Review result CSV.

    To run it again, click Review again. To clear it, click Clear.

Auto Email (overdue · due soon notices)

Step by step · Set up automatic email
  1. Click Auto Email.

    Settings are on the left, and the Preview by region (Vietnam (n) / India (n)) is on the right.

  2. Turn on Auto send and choose Repeat.

    Every day or Select days (several weekdays). Send time is Korean time. Items to send are overdue items + items due within [N] days (0–30 days).

  3. Expand To · CC and check them for each region.

    For Vietnam and India: send checkbox · greeting name · address chips.

  4. Check the mail template (English).

    The body must contain the {TABLES} placeholder. To go back to the original, click Reset template.

  5. Check with Test mail to me and click 💾 Save.

    Check the Next auto send time and the Recent send log.

  • Automatic sending happens once a day. Regions with nothing to send are skipped.
  • ✉ Send now sends right away with the saved settings, and replaces that day's automatic sending.

BOM Management

Sidebar › Operations › BOM ManagementAccess: BOM Management

Here you look up and correct which materials, and how many of each, make up a finished product (BOM), and at what price materials are bought (purchase price).

Top tabWhat it does
BOM (bill of materials)Shows parent · child items, required quantity, process, M/C and customer, one row each.
Purchase price listShows purchase prices by item (MAX · MIN · AVG) and their changes.
M-BOMProduct material cost — the total material cost to make one product (excluding paid supply materials)
E-BOM · F-BOMEngineering · finance BOMs, entered and managed by hand (F-BOM includes amount · currency · FX rate)
SettingsUpload BOM · price files, manage vendors

Find · correct materials

Step by step · BOM (bill of materials)
  1. Click the BOM (bill of materials) tab.

    The first time, a progress bar runs while the data downloads. After that, it opens right away.

  2. Enter a part number · part name · process name, etc. in the search box.

    Narrow down further with Type · Level · Customer. To clear all, click Reset. Results are split into pages of 50.

  3. Click a row to open the BOM detail specs window.

    Parent · child information, process · production specs, notes. Click the customer name to see the business partner information.

  4. To correct it: Edit → change the values → Save.
  5. If needed, download the currently filtered list with Excel.

    File name: BOM자재명세서_날짜.xlsx (BOM bill of materials_date)

  • If you correct the child unit (UM), it is remembered separately for that part number and kept even when the BOM is uploaded again. It is shown in orange text in the table. If you empty the field, it goes back to the original unit.
  • Level 1 has a green tag, level 2 a blue tag. Required quantity = the number needed for one parent item.
Delete in the detail window removes that row from the database completely. Before deleting, check the parent · child part numbers once more.

Purchase price · Product material cost

  • Purchase price list: search box (item code · item name · vendor name · spec) + vendor · currency · type selectors → within the results, narrow down once more with Abbreviation · Item kind. Details → price information → Edit → Save. Excel downloads the narrowed list as is.
  • M-BOM (product material cost): expand a main category (project) and choose a product to see the total material cost to make one. Adjust prices in a different unit with Apply price formula (× 10, ÷ 100, etc.); this is kept even after re-uploading. To remove it, click Remove formula.

Upload BOM · price files

Step by step · Upload the BOM
  1. Click Settings at the top right of BOM Management → go to data upload.

    This is Settings inside BOM Management, not Settings › Upload on the main screen.

  2. For the BOM, upload one Frm_MASTER BOM file in BOM upload.
  3. For prices: Select finished-product file · Select semi-finished file → Upload prices (merge finished + semi-finished).

    Get the template with Template. Only the uploaded kinds are replaced, and child units you changed by hand are kept.

Materials · Getting started

Sidebar › Operations › MaterialsAccess: Materials (Email Settings · Data Check: Master)

Takes the 8/31 ledger stock, adds MES receipts · production actuals, and calculates future usage from BOM × production plan · rolling S/L. It tells you which material runs out when, and when you must order it.

Screen layout · top to bottom
  1. Top menuDashboard · Materials · PO Planning · Free Issue Requests · Production Risk Check · Ledger baseline · Email Settings · Data Check · on the right, Recalculate
  2. Title barScreen name · Export (CSV)
  3. Search conditionsDate basis · Material type · Item group · Customer · Supplier · Excl. JG · Excl. goods (Dashboard Stock · Runout, Production Risk Check, Ledger baseline)
  4. Data barChips for ledger · BOM · production plan · VF rolling S/L · production actuals · MES receipts · import material list (green = present, red = missing) and the data dates

Numbers on the top menu: Dashboard = number of out-of-stock + urgent materials; Materials = number of focus items (inflator · OPW · wire harness) that need action; Free Issue Requests = requests waiting for confirmation · late; Production Risk Check = number of products that cannot be produced.

Daily order of work

Step by step · A day in Materials
  1. (Data owner) Upload the MES data.

    Settings › Upload › the 4 Materials files (purchases · outsourced processing · goods sales · overseas shipments). → Upload › Materials MES

  2. Click Recalculate at the top right.

    Click it once after uploading to recalculate with the latest data. Check that the dates on the data bar are recent.

  3. Look at the Dashboard › Stock · Runout cards.

    Check the counts for urgent procurement · order this week · order within 4 weeks. → Dashboard

  4. Open urgent materials from the Materials list.

    Check the expected run-out · order timing, and if needed, register planned receipts or confirm receipts. → Material list · Detail

  5. In PO Planning, enter the monthly order plan and send it to suppliers.

    → PO Planning

  6. (Production Control) Handle requests waiting for confirmation · late in Free Issue Requests.

    Confirm sample · take-out requests and process them through to issue. → Free Issue Requests

Choose the search conditions

  • Date basis: Today (default) or the ledger closing date (example: 8/31 closing). It is also used for the Materials calculation.
  • Material type: All / Overseas (import) / Domestic. Item group: All materials · Key materials · ★ Focus items · ★ Inflator · ★ OPW · ★ Wire harness · VINFAST · KGM shared · overseas lead time 90 · 60 · 45 days or more.
  • Excl. JG · Excl. goods are checked by default.
  • Type · customer · supplier use the same values on all screens. If you change them in one place, the other screens change too.
  • Filter fields that are not at their default value turn green, and ↺ Reset appears.

What the statuses mean · How dates are set

StatusMeaning · What to do
Out of stockStock on hand is 0 or less — procure immediately
UrgentExpected to run out within 14 days — check the receipt · procurement schedule first
Order neededExpected to run out within the lead time (the reorder date is today or past) — place an order
Receipt check neededNormal only if the planned receipt arrives, or the planned date has passed and it has not been cleared — check the actual receipt (Materials list only)
NormalNo order warning within the current calculation range
Stock unconfirmedNot in the ledger and no stock count set — check the base data
DormantNo usage in the last 30 days · next 60 days — check whether production has stopped (materials used for free issues are not dormant · you can change it yourself with the 💤 Dormant stock switch in the detail)
Discontinued after run-outOnly the remaining stock is used and it is not reordered — left out of alerts · orders (the Stop after run-out switch in the detail)
Date · NumberHow it is calculated
Stock on hand8/31 ledger closing + later MES receipts · in-house production − usage (if a stock count was set, from that day's quantity)
UsageProduction actuals × BOM; future days use MES production plan · rolling S/L × BOM (materials with no BOM use the August average; goods use goods sales)
Expected run-outThe first day the stock reaches 0 or less, subtracting each future day's usage (within 365 days). Now = without planned receipts · With receipts = adding them on the planned dates
Reorder dateExpected run-out (with receipts) − lead time (lead time = value edited on screen → item master → default 60 days overseas · 30 days domestic)
Additional order dateNext run-out date after all planned receipts arrive − lead time
Next · Materials Dashboard →
21.1

Materials Dashboard

Materials › Dashboard

The top tabs show four things: Production · Shipments · Supplier Purchases · Outsourcing · Stock · Runout · PO plan. At first, the current month is shown.

Stock · Runout — what runs out when

Step by step · Find urgent materials
  1. Click the Stock · Runout tab and look at the 4 cards at the top.

    Urgent procurement (out of stock) · Order this week (recommended order date within 7 days · including past ones) · Order within 4 weeks (8–28 days) · Dormant · no stock. 📦 Ordered N is shown below the cards.

  2. Click a card to open a list window.

    Click Filter the table below by this in the window to narrow the table below to that condition.

  3. Look at the Next 12 weeks · materials running out by week bars.

    Blue = overseas, gray = domestic, ★ = focus item. Click a bar to show only that week; clear it with Clear week ✕.

  4. Narrow down with the status bar above the table or the filters.

    Click a section of the status bar to show only that status; click again to show all. Search · Status · Usage basis · In use only.

  5. Click a row to see details in the side window.

    Base stock · estimated stock · receipts · daily usage · 120-day expected stock line · products that use this material.

Recommended order columnMeaning
📦 Ordered + receipt MM/DDA planned receipt is set = already ordered. Check that it arrived on the planned date
⚠ Check late receiptThe planned receipt date has passed
🚚 Shipped MM/DD · 🗓 Departs MM/DD · ⚠ Departure MM/DD passedSupplier shipping record — whether the goods have left · whether the planned departure date has passed → Supplier shipping record

Production · Shipments · Supplier purchases · PO plan

Cards (production quantity · daily average · part numbers · defect rate), charts by date · top 10, and a part-number table. Click a row to see a chart by date and the BOM materials.
  • Supplier Purchases · Outsourcing: Data Purchases / Outsourced processing. Click a row to see receipts by part number.
  • PO plan: Period start ~ Period end · Quick period (FIRM / FORECAST / 10 months / last 6 months) · vendor · item. Monthly bars (green FIRM · purple FORECAST · gray PAST) and an expected usage line. Click a supplier · material row to open that supplier · part number in PO Planning.

Click a table header to sort. Choose 50 / 100 / 200 rows per page.

21.2

Material list · Material detail

Materials › Materials

For each material, check when it runs out · when to order · whether the receipt arrived, and record planned receipts · receipt confirmations. ★ Inflator · OPW · wire harness come first.

List screen · top to bottom
  1. HeaderRunout & planned receipts by material · count · status bar · ⚠ Late receipts N · Clean up · 🔁 Part number change rules N
  2. FiltersSearch · item group (default ★ Focus items) · type · customer · supplier · status · order timing · ↺ Reset
  3. Run-out timelineThe 10 most urgent materials as 120-day bars (▼ order date · ◆ planned receipt)
  4. TablePart number · part name · parent part number (FG) · supplier │ current stock │ 1st planned receipt │ 2nd planned receipt │ lead time · order · status

Find urgent materials

Step by step · Narrow down the list
  1. In the status bar, click the Out of stock · Urgent section.

    Only that status remains. Click again to go back to all.

  2. In Order timing, choose Reorder date passed · Order within 7 days.

    To see only materials already ordered, choose 📦 Waiting for receipt (ordered).

  3. Enter a part number · part name · parent part number · supplier in the search box.

    Part numbers are found even without - or spaces (example: 569036AA26 = 569036-AA26). If nothing is found, the reason (example: not in the item group) and Open this material → are shown.

  4. Click a row to open the material detail in full screen.

    When you come back with ← Back to list · Esc · the browser Back button, the filters and scroll position stay the same.

  • Color groups in the table: gray-blue = current stock (on hand · expected run-out), light green = 1st planned receipt, light purple = 2nd planned receipt. If the run-out date is the same even after adding the receipt, it shows No change (the receipt comes after the run-out).
  • Click +N in the parent part number column to expand all of them in place. Copy copies all of them with line breaks. Dragging over text to copy part of it does not open the detail.
  • Click + Add or a date in a receipt column to register · edit a planned receipt or confirm a receipt in a small window, without opening the detail.
  • Dormant materials are collapsed in ▸ 💤 N dormant materials … Expand at the bottom. If the judgment is wrong, change it in the detail → Dormant stock · Stop after run-out
  • Small marks next to the 1st · 2nd receipt dates: 🚚 shipment confirmed · 🗓 planned departure · ⚠ planned departure passed → Supplier shipping record

How to read the material detail

Detail screen · top to bottom
  1. HeaderPart number · status · tags · part name · supplier · parent part number · two switches on the right: 💤 Dormant stock (Auto / Manual) · Stop after run-out (no reorder)
  2. Order barOne line on what to do now (example: ⚠ Reorder date passed N days ago — order now · 🛒 D-N until order) · if there is a planned receipt, the 📦 Ordered · waiting for receipt bar and a time bar · a supplier shipping mark on each line (🚚 Shipped · 🗓 Departs MM/DD · ⚠ Not shipped)
  3. 6 number cardsStock on hand · average daily usage · expected run-out now · expected run-out with receipts · lead time (✎ Edit) · reorder date (or order status)
  4. Stock run-out chartDaily / Weekly / Monthly · ◀ period ▶
  5. View detailsTwo cards you click to switch: 📦 Receipts · Stock records · 📅 Stock by period
ChartMeaning
Purple lineCurrent stock (without planned receipts). After today, dotted line = forecast
Teal dotted lineStock if planned receipts also arrive
Green bar / light green outlineReceipt / planned receipt
Orange bars in the lower areaQuantity used that day (week · month) — it has its own scale, so small quantities are visible too
Vertical linesToday · expected run-out (red) · order needed (or additional order)
▶ Triangle (filled / white)Supplier departure date — filled = 🚚 shipment confirmed · white = planned departure (not shipped)
Departure → receipt arrow lineFrom the departure date to the planned receipt date (solid = shipment confirmed · dotted = planned departure)
Do not judge that material has already arrived just from the teal dotted line. A plan is not an actual receipt. When the planned date passes, check whether it arrived and click Confirm receipt.

Edit the lead time

Step by step · Edit the lead time
  1. On the Lead time card in the detail, click ✎ Edit.

    The card shows where the current value comes from (item master · default · ✎ edited).

  2. Enter the number of days (1 – 365).

    While you type, the new reorder date is previewed.

  3. Click Save (or press Enter).

    Reorder date, status, dashboard and email are recalculated with the new value, and a ✎ mark appears in the list, dashboard and PO Planning. To go back, use ↺ Back to ….

Change Dormant stock · Stop after run-out

Use the two switches on the right of the detail header to take a material out of alerts · orders, or put it back. When you switch, the screen, automatic email and AI search recalculate with the same judgment.

SwitchWhen onUse it when
💤 Dormant stockTreated as dormant — left out of order alerts · email · the default list view, and moved to the 💤 group at the bottom of the list. When off, it is not dormant (subject to out-of-stock · order alerts)Production has ended or the material is not used for now · or the opposite: it is used but was wrongly judged dormant
Stop after run-out (no reorder)Only the remaining stock is used and it is not reordered — left out of out-of-stock · urgent · order-needed alerts, the mail order tables and PO plan auto-fill; only the expected run-out date is shownMaterials to be discontinued · replaced by another part number (the 15 Jinyoung Electric wire harnesses are on from the start)
Step by step · Set dormant stock yourself
  1. Open the material detail and look at the 💤 Dormant stock switch on the right of the header.

    At the end of the switch, Auto = the system's judgment, Manual = set by someone. Hover over it to see the automatic judgment and the reason (example: 0 usage in the last 30 days · 0 demand in the next 60 days), and who set it · when.

  2. Turn the switch on or off.

    On shows Marked as dormant stock; off shows Changed back to non-dormant stock. Materials you set as dormant show as 💤 Dormant (set manually) in the dashboard table.

  3. To go back to the automatic judgment, switch it back to the same side as the automatic judgment.

    The manual setting is cleared and the end of the switch shows Auto again.

Step by step · Stop after run-out
  1. Turn on the Stop after run-out (no reorder) switch on the right of the detail header.

    Marked as discontinued after run-out appears, the status becomes Discontinued after run-out, and the order cell in the list shows No reorder.

  2. To make it an order target again, turn the switch off.

    Discontinued-after-run-out mark removed — it is an order alert target again appears.

Planned receipt · Confirm receipt · Set stock count

Under View details at the bottom of the detail, click the 📦 Receipts · Stock records card. The fields for a new record are at the top, and the record list is below.

Step by step · Register a planned receipt (when you placed an order)
  1. From the kind buttons, choose 📦 Planned receipt.
  2. Enter Planned date · Qty, and write the PO number · shipping date, etc. in Memo (optional).
  3. Check the preview line below.

    If registered → expected run-out A → B (+N days) · reorder date C. If the receipt comes after the run-out, a ⚠ warning appears.

  4. Click + Register.

    The material is now marked 📦 Ordered, and a waiting-for-receipt bar appears at the top of the detail.

Step by step · Confirm receipt (when it actually arrived)
  1. Click ✓ Confirm receipt on the planned row (or on the bar at the top).
  2. Check or correct Actual date · Actual qty.

    The quantity that will actually be added to stock is previewed below. If there is an MES purchase within 7 days before the receipt date, ⚠ There is an MES purchase within 7 days before the receipt date appears. If that MES is a different receipt, choose Different receipt (adds the whole confirmed quantity); if it is the same receipt, choose Same receipt (adds only what MES has not already counted).

  3. Click Add to stock.

    To add a receipt directly without a plan: kind ✅ Confirm receipt → date (up to today) · quantity → + Add to stock.

Step by step · Set stock count (when you actually counted)
  1. Choose the kind 🔢 Set stock count.
  2. Enter Count date and Actual quantity on hand (0 is allowed).

    The preview shows the difference from the calculation.

  3. Click + Set stock count.

    From that day, counting starts again from this quantity.

  • Correct a record: Edit → correct it in the fields below → Save. Delete: Delete → confirm. To turn a confirmed receipt back into a plan, click Back to plan.
  • Saving shows on the screen right away, and the server save continues in the background. If it fails, Could not save, so the change was undone appears.

Matched with MES receipts automatically

MES purchases (Settings › Upload) are the basis for actual receipts. They are matched with the records on the screen automatically, so the same receipt is not counted twice.

MarkMeaning
MES N received · M leftN of the planned quantity arrived through MES; only the remaining M is used in future calculations
In MES ✓The confirmed receipt quantity is also in MES (counted only once)
Waiting for MESNot in MES yet — matched automatically when MES is uploaded
🔗 Auto-confirmed by MES: NList of planned receipts that fully arrived through MES (expand to check)
Different from earlier MESA receipt confirmed with Different receipt — added separately from the MES within 7 days before

Supplier shipping record (planned departure · shipment confirmed)

For each planned receipt, record the planned departure date the supplier gave you and the day it actually left (shipment confirmed), so you can see at a glance whether goods are on the way. The planned receipt date and the stock calculation do not change.

Step by step · Record a shipment
  1. In the detail's 📦 Receipts · Stock records, click 🚚 Shipping record on a planned receipt line.

    The 🚚 Supplier shipment fields open under the line. If it is already recorded, the button shows the current state (example: 🗓 Departs 11/20); click it to change.

  2. Enter the Planned departure date from the supplier and click Save.

    The state becomes 🗓 Departs MM/DD.

  3. When it actually leaves, enter Actual departure · Tracking no. · B/L (optional) and click Save.

    If it left today, just click 🚚 Shipped today. The state becomes 🚚 Shipped MM/DD. If you made a mistake, use Delete record.

MarkMeaning · what to do
🗓 Departs MM/DDDeparture date from the supplier · not yet confirmed as shipped
🚚 Shipped MM/DDActually left (shipment confirmed) · hover to see the tracking number · days from departure to receipt
⚠ Departure MM/DD passedThe planned departure date has passed with no shipment confirmation — check with the supplier whether it was actually sent

Where it shows: next to the 1st · 2nd receipt dates in the material list · the waiting-for-receipt bar at the top of the detail · the stock run-out chart (▶ departure date · departure → receipt line) · the order recommendation cell in Dashboard › Stock · Runout table · the ordered line in the automatic email.

Clean up late receipts · Part number change

Step by step · Clean up late planned receipts
  1. Click ⚠ Late receipts N · Clean up in the list header.

    Material · planned date · quantity · MES receipts · memo are shown. If MES receipts are 80% or more, Seems to have arrived is added.

  2. Choose the rows to process.

    Select all or Select N with MES receipt confirmed.

  3. Click the right button.
    • ✓ Confirm receipt for N selected (add to stock): when it actually arrived but is not in MES
    • N selected: in MES: when it is already in MES
    • Delete N selected · to change only the date, use Edit date on the row
  • 🔁 Part number change rules: Record when a product's material part number changed because the supplier changed. Effective date · product · new part number · part number to replace (empty = automatic) · memo → + Add row → Save · recalculate. From the effective date, usage is counted under the new part number (old stock is not moved to the new part number).
  • Stop after run-out: the switch in the detail header that makes a material use only its remaining stock with no reorder. → Dormant stock · Stop after run-out
Next · PO Planning · Send SL to suppliers →
21.3

PO Planning · Send SL to suppliers

Materials › PO Planning

Enter the monthly order plan (SL) by material and send it to suppliers by Excel · email. FIRM = 4 months including this month, FORECAST = the 6 months after that. Example: in November, FIRM is November–February and FORECAST is March–August. When sending to suppliers, choose the period to send (up to 12 months) and the language (Korean / English).

Step by step · Enter the order plan
  1. Narrow down the materials with the filters.

    Search · View from (10 months from that month) · item group (default ★ Focus items) · supplier · type · customer · With plan only.

  2. Click ✎ Edit.
  3. Enter quantities in the month fields.

    Faded numbers are the expected usage for that month. ≈ marks an estimate from the average of the last 3 months, for months after the plan · S/L ends. Enter = field below, Tab = next field, Esc = undo that field.

  4. Click Save (N).

    All changed fields are saved together. To stop, click Cancel (it asks whether to undo everything). Changed fields are kept even if you move to another page.

Auto-fill · Clear range · Memo

  • ⚡ Auto-fill: Which materials (whole screen / this page / materials with a plan) · Which months (FIRM · FORECAST · 10 months) · Which cells (empty only / overwrite) · fill with 3-month average · Include VINFAST backorder (% by month, total 100%) → Fill. It is not saved yet, so you must click Save (↶ Undo is possible).
  • 🧹 Clear range: choose materials · months to clear only the quantities (memos stay). This also needs Save to take effect.
  • 📝 Memo: material memo · memo per month · edit history. A cell with an orange dot has a memo for that month.

Send to suppliers

Step by step · SL Excel · email
  1. Filter by the supplier to send to, then click ⬇ SL Excel (for suppliers).

    The SL Excel · Email (for suppliers) window opens. The materials are exactly as filtered now (all pages).

  2. Choose the Period to send.

    Start month – end month (up to 12 months), or From this month FIRM 4 months · 6 months · 10 months. The default is 10 months from the View-from month. → Choose the period to send

  3. Check the Language.

    Overseas suppliers start with English, domestic suppliers with Korean. → Send an English SL

  4. Write the Message to supplier (optional).

    It goes at the top of the Excel file and into the email body.

  5. To get the file, click ⬇ Download Excel. To send it by email, click the envelope icon (Send by email).
  6. In the ✉ Send SL email window, check To (required) · CC · subject · body.

    You can also change the language · period in this window (your edited subject · body stay; only the period text moves to the new months). If you chose a supplier, check Save to the supplier's address book to reuse it next time. Body placeholders: {매입처} (supplier) {FIRM} {FORECAST} {기간} (period) {전할말} (message).

  7. Open the Excel file with the attachment's ⬇ Preview download, then click ✉ Send.

    The Sent log at the bottom of the window keeps the time · sender · recipients · subject · period (months · English) · result.

This SL email goes out only when you send it yourself. It is a different function from the internal procurement alert email (Email Settings).

Choose the period to send

Month cell colorMeaning
FIRM4 months including this month — confirmed orders (please keep the quantity · delivery date)
FORECASTThe months after that — forecast (for production · material planning, not confirmed)
PASTMonths before this month — past plans (included if you choose a start month before this month)
  • The period is up to 12 months. The end month can only be chosen within 12 months from the start month.
  • The downloaded Excel, the email attachment and {기간} (period) {FIRM} {FORECAST} in the email body all use the same months you chose. The months included are shown under the fields, like FIRM 2026-11 ~ 2027-02 (4).

Send an English SL to overseas suppliers

Step by step · English SL
  1. Filter by the overseas supplier and click ⬇ SL Excel (for suppliers).

    If at least half of that supplier's materials are overseas (imported), it shows Overseas supplier — English automatically (AI translation) and English is chosen first. Domestic suppliers can also get an English SL if you click English.

  2. You may write the message in Korean.

    With English, the subject, body and Excel (sheet name Schedule Line · headers Part No. · Description …) become English, and AI translates Korean messages · part names · memos into English.

  3. Read the subject · body in the email window.

    If ⚠ Korean text remains in the subject · body appears, click 🌐 AI English translation or fix it in English.

  4. Check the English Excel with ⬇ Preview download, then click ✉ Send.

    If N Korean texts in the Excel could not be translated appears after sending, Korean remains in the Excel, so check the part names · memos.

  • The default text is saved separately for Korean · English (💾 Save as default text · ↺ Original text). English placeholders: {SUPPLIER} {PERIOD} {MONTHS} {FIRM} {FORECAST} {NOTE}.
AI translation can be wrong. Part numbers, quantities and dates are kept as they are, but always read it once before sending.
Next · Free Issue Requests (samples · take-outs) →
21.4

Free Issue Requests (samples · take-outs)

Materials › Free Issue RequestsView · request: Materials access · Confirm · issue: Production Control team · master · Excel upload: master

Request materials or products that leave the company free of charge (samples, take-outs), and let the Production Control team confirm and issue them. This replaces the monthly “Sample & Take-out Request” Excel, and the results flow straight into Materials stock and planned receipts.

  • ① RequestRequester · + New request (part no. · quantity · purpose · needed date)
  • ② ConfirmProduction Control · method · available date · expected receipt → Materials planned receipt
  • ③ IssuedProduction Control · issued quantity · date · place → deducted from Materials stock
Screen layout · top to bottom
  1. 4 number cardsWaiting for confirmation · In progress · ⚠ Late (needed date passed, not issued yet) · Issued — click one to show only those requests
  2. HeaderFree issue requests · period · count · status bar · your access · + New request · 📤 Upload Excel (master)
  3. Production Control contactsPerson in charge of imported materials · domestic materials · finished goods (read from the top row of the Excel)
  4. FiltersSearch · Period (request form month) year · month ‹ › · status · division · material/product · issue type · requester · Only my items · my requests · ↺ Reset
  5. TableNo · part no. · part name · division · requested qty · needed date · issue type · available · planned receipt (Materials) · issue · Materials stock · contact · status · action — lines of the same request (requester · purpose · needed date) are grouped

It opens on this month. Use ‹ › to move between months and This month to come back. The number next to Free Issue Requests in the top menu is, for Production Control, the requests waiting for confirmation + late ones in progress; for everyone else, the late requests.

StatusMeaning · whose turn
WaitingRequest received — Production Control to confirm (the requester can still edit or delete it)
Confirmed · in progressProduction Control set the method and dates — to be issued
IssuedSent out — deducted from Materials stock
RejectedWill not be handled — the reason is kept in the log
⚠ LateThe sample needed date has passed and it is not issued yet

Make a request (requester)

Step by step · New free issue request
  1. Click + New request.

    The + New free issue request window opens. Put several part numbers into one request.

  2. At the top, enter Requester · Division · Vehicle · Sample needed date · Purpose.

    e.g. Purpose: KGM development validation sample · parts for JG rework

  3. On each line, choose Material/Product and enter the Part No..

    Enter just the part number and the name and unit fill in automatically and lock (✓ match). If the part number is unknown, ✗ Not in the item list and similar part numbers appear — pick one.

  4. Enter the Requested qty, and if needed the Issue type (preferred) and Remarks.

    Leave the issue type empty and Production Control will decide (warehouse stock · production request · receipt request). Add lines with + Add line; remove one with ✕ at the end of the line.

  5. Click Submit request.

    N request lines registered — waiting for Production Control. appears and the status is Waiting. If a part number exists nowhere, you are asked Submit as is / Fix part no..

  • Your own Waiting requests made on screen can be changed or removed with Edit · Delete on the line. After Production Control confirms, only they can change it.
  • Click a line to open the detail panel on the right: progress ① request → ② confirm → ③ issued, request details, Production Control handling, Materials stock and expected run-out for the same part, and the log (who · when).
  • To see only your own, tick Only my items · my requests.

Confirm · issue (Production Control)

Only people whose account department contains '생산관리' (Production Control) or '생관', and masters, can do this. You have the right if the header shows ✓ Production Control access.

Step by step · Confirm
  1. Click ✓ Confirm on a Waiting line.

    For several lines, tick the boxes on the left and use ✓ Confirm on the bar above. Ticking a group header selects the whole request.

  2. Choose the Method: warehouse stock · production request · receipt request.
  3. Enter the Available date, and for materials still to arrive, the Expected receipt date.

    For several lines, (Apply to all) fills the same date. Add a Memo if needed.

  4. On each line, check the Planned receipt qty and the Materials planned receipt Add switch.

    With Add on, it is added as a planned receipt (🎁 mark) for the same part in Materials. It is on by default for receipt requests. Part numbers not in the Materials list (finished goods etc.) show Not in list.

  5. Click ✓ Confirm.

    The status changes to Confirmed · in progress. To change dates, use Edit on the line.

Step by step · Issue
  1. Click 📦 Issue on an in-progress line.

    To issue a Waiting line straight away, use 📦 Issue now in the detail panel; for several lines, tick them and use 📦 Issued on the bar above.

  2. Enter the Issue date (up to today) and Issue place (warehouse · line).
  3. On each line, check the Issued qty and Deduct from stock.

    If left on, the issued quantity is entered as a minus in Materials Receipts · Stock records on the issue date, and stock and expected run-out change at once. If the quantity is text such as “1 BOX”, only a record is kept and nothing is deducted (enter a number to deduct).

  4. Click 📦 Issued.

    The status becomes Issued and the table's Issue column shows Stock −N.

  • Reject: Reject → reason (required) → Reject. To take it back, use Undo reject in the detail panel.
  • Undo (detail panel): Cancel confirmation = back to Waiting (removes the planned receipt that was added automatically · receipts already confirmed stay) · Cancel issue = back to in progress (removes the deduction, so the stock comes back).
Delete in the detail panel also removes the linked Materials planned receipt and stock record, and cannot be undone.

Upload the request form Excel (master)

Step by step · Upload the “Sample & Take-out Request”
  1. Click 📤 Upload Excel and drop the files.

    You can pick several files at once. The month in the file name (e.g. 2026 10월 무상출고.xlsx → October) is used as the request month.

  2. Check the preview for each file.

    It shows new · changed · same counts and warnings (cells whose date could not be read, etc.). Open the lines with ▸ Show lines.

  3. Click Save.

    Uploading again updates the same lines (month · No · part no.) and only adds new ones. Values Production Control handled on screen are not overwritten. Issued lines can also be reflected in stock with Deduct issued lines from Materials stock.

The “item info” sheet in the same file is saved too, so in the + New request window, entering a part number fills the same name and unit as the Excel VLOOKUP.

Where it connects to Materials

Materials screenWhat you see
Material list🎁 N next to the part no. = N free issue requests not handled yet
Material detailThe 🎁 Free issue requests band (open · issued · quantity deducted) · View in Free Issue Requests →
Planned receiptsPlans added at confirmation carry a 🎁 number tag — you may edit the date and quantity in Materials
Receipts · Stock recordsThe 🎁 Free issue group = minus quantities deducted when issued (edit · undo in Free Issue Requests)
Status (Dormant)Materials used for free issues (open requests · issued in the last 90 days) are not treated as dormant, even with no production or purchase usage

Production Risk Check · Ledger baseline

Materials › Production Risk Check · Ledger baseline

Finds, for each finished product, the day production is blocked by a material shortage. Ledger baseline shows the Finance & Accounting team's ledger, the starting point of the stock calculation, as it is.

Production Risk Check

Step by step · Find blocked products
  1. In the status bar, click Cannot produce or Urgent.

    Future plan only is on by default. You can also narrow down with search (product · line · material part number) · Line · Status.

  2. Look at the table.

    Materials OK (bar + short N) · Bottleneck material · buildable alone · Shortage starts · future plan · Blocked plan qty. Click ▶ to expand the material rows.

  3. Click a row to open the product detail.

    Chart (dark teal actuals · light teal production plan · light purple VINFAST rolling S/L · red blocked plan), Production plan by date (can produce / partly possible / cannot produce), Linked materials (BOM).

  4. Click a bottleneck material row to see the material information, and handle receipts · orders in Materials.

    To go back, click ← Back to list.

For the detailed judgment rules, expand How to read Production Risk Check at the bottom of the screen.

Ledger baseline

Shows the end-of-August ledger (base stock) by part number. Narrow down with search (part number · part name · vendor) and Sheet, and see the number of part numbers · closing quantity · closing amount in the title bar.
