21.6

Email Settings · Phone alerts · Data Check

Materials › Email Settings · Data CheckAccess: Master

Automatically sends procurement · order alert emails to internal staff on set days · times, and sends voice messages to phones.

Email Settings screen
  1. Top 4 boxesAuto send (on/off · next send) · Last sent · Data basis (latest / ⚠ check) · Materials in the current email (of which 📦 ordered N)
  2. ① Auto send · schedule3 groups: All / Overseas imports only / ★ Focus items
  3. ② Content · ③ Recipients · ④ Mail textChoose tables · internal recipients · CC · subject · body · signature
  4. Right · bottomPreview (PC/Phone) · phone alerts · voice messages · send log
Step by step · Set up automatic email
  1. Turn on the Auto send switch at the very top.
  2. In ①, set the schedule switch · time · Every day or Select days for each group.

    ◉ marks the group used for preview · test · send now. The same group is sent only once a day. If there is nothing to send, it is logged as Skipped.

  3. In ②, choose the tables to include.

    Urgent procurement · order this week · order within 4 weeks. The overseas + domestic choice is used only for the All group.

  4. In ③, expand and check the recipients.

    All three groups go to the same people. Search by name · ID · part of an address and press Enter. Use ⇄ or drag to move between To ↔ CC, and × to remove.

  5. Preview → check with Test mail to me → Save.

    To send right away, click Send now (◉ group · today's automatic send for that group is skipped).

If ⚠ appears in Data basis, production actuals · MES receipts have not been uploaded for more than 3 days, or the MES production plan ends within 7 days, or the calculation date differs from today. The numbers in the email may be different from reality, so upload the data first.
  • The mail tables show materials with planned receipts separately in a 📦 Ordered · confirm receipt group at the bottom (the count is the same as on the dashboard cards).
  • The ordered line in the email also shows the supplier shipment (🚚 shipped · planned departure · ⚠ planned departure passed). → Supplier shipping record
  • Phone alerts · Voice messages: The AKC Android app reads out the urgent procurement items. Default 08:30 · 17:30, including weekends. Save · Listen · Test on my phone. Sent to the registered devices of people with Materials access.
  • Send log: the last 20 sends (time · kind · result sent/skipped/failed · content).

Data Check

Shows with a score whether the data used in the calculation is well connected (80% or more green · 50% or more yellow · below that red). See the cards by data, Materials with large August differences, Materials with different units, Planned parts without BOM, Find part number (why a part number is not shown), and the 7 items of Omission risk check. When numbers look wrong, check here first.

Sales ML (Sales master list)

Sidebar › Operations › Sales MLAccess: Sales ML

View and edit the Sales Team master list Excel (bid status · approval status · development · mass production status · price status · JG lump-sum tooling cost) in the same layout as the Excel file. Edited values are visible to everyone right away.

Top menu (SALES ML)

  2. Bid list · New entry · Subtotal · Notes managementFind, edit and add rows of the 1.Bid Status sheet

Legend below the table: waiting to save · save failed · merged cell / formula cell (cannot be edited) · faded value = taken over from the row above · #REF! = error in the original. On a phone, the selectors are folded inside Show filters (n) ▾.

Tooling Cost ICIMS · Overview

On one screen, you ask a supplier to enter tooling factors, review · approve the values and evidence photos the supplier entered through a link, and register them in ICIMS. Changes appear right away on the screens of everyone who has it open.

Teams · Access

  • If your account department contains 'Sales', you see the Sales Team tab; if it contains 'Purchasing', you see the Purchasing Team tab (Masters see both). The number next to a tab = number of items where it is your turn.
  • Anyone can use New request · Cost standards. Only Masters · Admins can use Import Excel.
  • People whose department contains Purchasing · Sales, and admins, can edit values (header shows Editable / View only).

List screen

Step by step · Find my tasks
  1. Click the Sales Team or Purchasing Team tab.
  2. Click one of the My tasks buttons.

    To review · Returned after rejection · To approve · To register in ICIMS · Links to resend, etc. Clicking a number card at the top also filters by that status.

    In the Purchasing Team tab, click a supplier card (status ratio bar) to see only that supplier.

  4. Click a row to open the detail screen.

    Items with the most recent news come first. Check the marks next to the rows: My turn · Rejected · AI n (number of AI-read fields not yet checked).

How to read the detail screen

Detail screen
  1. Top bar← Back to list (keeps your filters) · Jump to section
  2. HeaderStatus · editable · submission deadline · part number · part name · process · supplier · Download Excel
  3. LeftStage display → groups of input fields (basic info · by process · additional mold info · calculation basis · core · location drawing · electrode · photos · drawings) → records (import · edit history · progress log, collapsed at first)
  4. 'What to do now' card on the rightWhose turn it is · why it is blocked · action buttons · Save changes (always expanded)
  5. Collapsed items on the rightAssign mold code · input validation · owner · supplier shared link · ICIMS registration · export · back to a previous stage
After editing values, click Save changes first; only then do the action buttons work. While you click an action button, it is locked as Processing…, so it cannot be clicked twice.
Next · Create a request · Send the link →
23.1

Create a request · Send the link

Tooling Cost ICIMS › New requestSales · Purchasing

AKC enters only the supplier · process · owner. The supplier enters the tooling factors through the link.

Step by step · New request
  1. Click New request.

    Progress shown at the top: Supplier → Part number → Owner → Send.

  2. 1. Supplier: enter a name · contact person · phone number in the search box and click Select ›.

    If it is not there: Register new supplier → Register and select. After selecting, you can correct the phone number · email with Edit.

  3. 2. Part number: choose the process (required).

    Injection · Extrusion · CHECKER · PRESS steel · PROGRESSIVE · Cold forging. Part No · Part Name · car model · production site · EO · SEQ · SUB · submission deadline (14 days later if empty) · message to the supplier are inside Show more fields.

  4. 3. Owner: choose Sales Team (default) / Purchasing Team and click a name.

    You can choose the approver (Sales Team) now, or leave it empty and choose at review confirmation. If you choose someone without access, a warning appears.

  5. Click Send request + shared link.

    The Supplier shared link created window appears: Copy · Preview supplier screen ↗ · Send by email · Close.

Send the link by email

Step by step · Email the link to the supplier
  1. Click Send by email in the link window, or, in Supplier shared link on the right of the detail, click Email.
  2. Check the recipients.

    The supplier's registered email is filled in by default. Add more with Enter; drag or use ⇄ to move to CC. If you need a copy, check Copy to me.

  3. Check the subject · body and correct them if needed.

    If the link address is missing from the body, you cannot send (Re-insert link · Restore original text).

  4. Click Send.

    The sender name is 'AKC Supplier Portal', and replies come to your email. It is recorded in the progress log.

  • The link stays open for 15 days from the day it was created. In Supplier shared link on the right of the detail, you can see the address again any time and Copy it. To make a new one, click Create new link (revoke old link); to block it, click Revoke link.
  • ✎ Edit default template (Purchasing · Sales · Admin): the mail template everyone shares. Click Available fields ({받는분} recipient · {품번} part number · {링크기한} link deadline · {서명} signature, etc.) to insert them, then click Save default template. {링크} (link) must be included. To go back to the original template, click Restore built-in template.
Next · Supplier input screen (shared link) →
23.2

Supplier input screen (shared link)

Link received by the supplier · no login

This is the screen the supplier's contact person sees. Show this chapter when you explain to a supplier how to use it.

Step by step · Supplier input
  1. Open the link.

    At the top, you see the request information, This link is valid until YYYY-MM-DD (D-n), and the AKC owner's requests. The process cannot be changed.

  2. Upload evidence images with Photo next to a field.

    CATIA measurement screenshots · drawings, etc. The AI reads the numbers, puts them in purple fields, and opens a photo check window. JPG · PNG · WEBP · HEIC · PDF.

  3. Check the values in the check window.

    Choose one of the Values read from photo, click Re-read with AI, or correct it yourself. Remove a wrong photo with Remove this photo. Move with ◀ Previous · Next ▶.

  4. Fill in the rest, using the input guide 🔍 next to each field.

    The nozzle count · offset core · EDM cost fields have Attach mold drawing · Attach offset core photo · Attach evidence. If the EDM cost is over 2, evidence is required to submit.

  5. Enter the name of the person submitting in the bottom bar and click Save draft or Submit.

    The bar shows Required n / N · red fields n. If there are red fields, it cannot be submitted. After submission, the same link becomes a read-only 'Submitted' screen.

  • Product width · length · height · NET weight are shown without decimals; projected area · thickness are shown to one decimal place. Photos are reduced in size automatically when saved.
  • When a revision is requested, the same link opens again, the reason is shown at the top, and the values entered before remain.
Next · Review · Approval · ICIMS registration →
23.3

Review · Approval · ICIMS registration

Tooling Cost ICIMS › Detail › What to do nowOwner · Approver

Review (owner)

Step by step · Confirm review / Request revision
  1. Click a purple Check needed field and compare the photo with the value.

    If they match, click Matches the photo — confirmed. All values read by the AI must be checked before you can confirm the review. The reasons for blocking are shown in the 'What to do now' card and in Input validation.

  2. If you corrected values, click Save changes.

    An edit history is kept for each field.

  3. Choose the name chip of the approver (Sales) to hand over to.

    If you choose yourself, you can approve right after.

  4. Review confirmed → name → Hand over.

    If something must be fixed, write the reason and click Request revision → supplier. The same link opens again (if 2 days or fewer remain, a new link window appears; send the new link).

Approve · Reject (approver)

Step by step · Approve
  1. In Assign mold code on the right, choose a code and click Save mold code.

    CHECKER · PRESS steel · PROGRESSIVE · Cold forging need a code to be approved. For injection · extrusion, type it yourself; they can be approved without it.

  2. Click Approve.

    To send it back, write the reason and click Reject → back to review. It returns to the owner as 'Rejected · re-review'.

ICIMS registration · Excel

Step by step · ICIMS registered
  1. In ICIMS registration · Export on the right, click Copy in ICIMS input order.
  2. Paste into the ICIMS screen and register.

    If needed, also download ICIMS input values Excel.

  3. ICIMS registered → Registered.

Download Excel in the header gives the supplier templates (tooling factor sheet · management ledger · history card · checklist) including photos. If there are many photos, it downloads after Preparing photos for Excel n/N.

Go back · Change owner

  • Back to a previous stage: in the last item on the right, choose the stage to go back to (supplier revision / review confirmed / waiting for approval / approved · before ICIMS registration) → owner of that stage (required) → reason (required) → Back to ○○ stage. This works even after ICIMS registration.
  • Take it yourself: in the What to do now card, Take it (owner) / Take it (approver) → Take.
  • Transfer owner: in the Owner item, Transfer owner → which role · who takes over · handover memo → Hand over.

Cost standards · Import Excel

Tooling Cost ICIMS › Cost standards · Import Excel

Look up cost standards

Step by step · Find a standard
  1. Click the Cost standards tab.

    Document buttons: PLASTIC injection standard · Input-factor manual.

  2. Enter a word in the search box (example: EDM cost, slide CORE).

    It searches both documents together, highlights matches in yellow and shows the count for each document.

  3. Move with the table of contents on the left (on a phone, the horizontal bar at the top), and open mold types with Expand ▼.

    Original p.N is the page number of the paper document.

Import Excel (Master · Admin)

  1. Choose the type and click Choose folder or Choose files (multiple). If needed, check 'Upload photos too'.
  2. Check the content in the list with View, then click Register all checked files (n). To stop partway, click Stop.
  3. For imported items, check the process · part number · process steps in the detail. For injection · extrusion items, Link original photos in the header fills only the photo items that are empty in the original Excel.
Next · EDD Management →
24

EDD Management

Sidebar › Operations › EDD ManagementAccess: EDD Management

Manages billing and payments of development cost · tooling cost · ED&D by project. When a payment deadline is near, it warns you with colors.

Screen layout · top to bottom
  1. Project tabs (top right)VF8 · VF9 · VF6 · VF7 Project
  2. Tool barSearch (part name · PO · reporter) · status · currency · sort · Project · Category · Add item
  3. Category tabsVinFast (revenue) · Dayou AP · Komos · DH Autolead (cost)
  4. TablePart / Desc · Reporter · Documents (PO · PPAP) · INV issue date · Total Cost (PAID/UNPAID) · Payment Schedule bar
Step by step · Register an EDD item · Confirm payment
  1. Click Add item or click an existing row.

    The Edit item window opens.

  2. Enter the basic information.

    Section · Reporter · Part Name (required) · Description · Release Date · PPAP · INV ISSUE (issue date) · PO Number · amount (USD or KRW).

  3. Set the payment installments.

    1st to 4th with Add stage / Remove stage. Each installment has a Ratio (%) and a Payment Date. +45 / +60 next to the date fills the planned date based on the INV issue date (enter the INV issue date first).

  4. Click Save.
  5. When the money actually arrives, click 👆 Confirm payment for that installment and save.

    It changes to ✓ Paid, and the list bar fills with green by that amount. Click again to go back to unpaid.

Entering a ratio · date does not mean it is paid. Even if you enter all 100%, it stays UNPAID until you click Confirm payment.
Payment due = INV issue date + 45 daysMark
8 days or more leftPayment due D-n
Within 7 daysPayment due D-n
Within 3 daysPayment due D-n!
PassedOverdue n days!
No INV issue dateINV not issued · installment n only
Next · Export Performance →
25

Export Performance

Sidebar › Operations › Export PerformanceAccess: Export Performance (upload · edit: Master · Admin)

Upload the Korea Customs Service export performance records, and they are sorted automatically by customer (main category) · item (sub-category) · fiscal month · week to show export results. The unit is KRW (₩).

Screen layout · top to bottom
  1. Title barFiscal year (FY = April to March of the next year) · month · Monthly/Weekly · Download for report · Data Upload
  2. TabsDashboard (total revenue · this month's revenue · top main category · trend · share) / Detail table (main category · item × weekly W1–W5 by month · A/S · TOTAL)
Step by step · Upload the customs file (admin)
  1. Click Data Upload.

    The Export data upload window opens on the right.

  2. Drag and drop the customs Excel file, or click Click to select.

    Several files are OK. Even if you upload 2 weeks at once, the data is split by date.

  3. Click Send upload.

    If it overlaps a period already uploaded, in Check overlapping period, choose Overwrite (only the old upload for that period is replaced) or Cancel.

  4. Check the result.

    The numbers added · skipped and Excluded from totals: n (items that are not government export performance, example: NBH, the Japanese head office) are shown.

Step by step · Enter domestic revenue by hand (Edit mode)
  1. In Detail table, click Edit mode at the far right.
  2. Enter numbers in the weekly fields of CKD · SB ASSY · DS ASSY · KGM · HKMC · Tata Daewoo, etc.

    Only numbers can be entered, and commas are added automatically. Edited fields are marked and n edited is shown. For a new row, click Add Row.

  3. Click Save.

    Saved. To stop, click Cancel.

  • Rows filled from the customs file, such as VINFAST · Global Parts, and subtotals · totals have a 🔒 mark and cannot be edited.
  • If a field has both a customs value and an edited value, a small orange dot appears. In Edit mode, choose for each field which to use: Edited n / Customs n (saved right away).
  • Download for report downloads all months of the selected fiscal year as a main category × month table (cumulative amount · ratio).
Next · Upload →
26

Upload

Sidebar › System › Settings › UploadAccess: Upload (Master · Admin by default)

This is the starting point of all screens. The other screens are built from the Excel files uploaded here. Once you upload a file, the change appears on every screen that uses that data.

Screen layout · top to bottom
  1. Title · StatusData Upload & Processing · status badge (Waiting → Processing → Done/Failed) · overall progress bar
  2. Three rows of tabs on the left① Weekly S/L · Production plan · Shipment data · Production actuals · Stock count · Price management ② Integrated Forecast: CKD · GLOBAL · Hyundai · Kia · KGM · Integrated price · CKD price ③ Materials MES: Purchases · Outsourced processing · Goods sales · Overseas shipments
  3. Log window on the rightSystem Terminal - Log Output — what was uploaded and when, one line each (30 days · last 3,000 lines)
  4. Bottom (Master · Admin)Main screen popup videos · export performance week settings · export performance weekly file upload

How to upload a file (same for all tabs)

Step by step · Upload a file
  1. Click the tab for the data you want to upload.

    If you do not know the format, get an empty file with an example from Download template in the tab.

  2. Drag and drop the file onto the dotted box Click or drop a file here.

    The file name and the period · target month read from it appear below the box. If the file is a different kind from the tab, the Check upload file window appears and it is not uploaded.

  3. Check the options for the tab.

    See the table below (especially Replace that month only).

  4. Click the colored button … Start loading → Proceed in the confirmation window.
  5. Wait while watching the progress bar and the log window on the right.

    While loading, other tabs · menus are locked. Do not close the window.

  6. When the Done window appears, you are done.

    All open screens change to the new data automatically.

What to upload in each tab

TabFile to upload · What to checkStart button
Weekly S/LCustomer's weekly order (once a week). First, in Target Region, choose Vietnam / India. The year and Snapshot Date (MM-DD) are filled from the file name. If the same date exists, it asks whether to Overwrite.Save to database
Production planMonthly production plan (once a month). Only that month is replaced; shared by Vietnam · India.Start loading production plan
Shipment dataActual shipment results (any time). Upload with Replace That Month Only (Cumulative) turned on.Start loading shipment data
Production actualsGood-part production quantities (any time). Same method as shipment data.Start loading production actuals
Stock countWarehouse stock count results. Check Count Date and start. It shows the Simulation result (rows · part numbers · quantities by customer), then click Proceed. Customers are split automatically by part number.Start loading stock count
Price managementEdit directly in the table, with no file (see below).—
CKD · GLOBALSales plan whose sheet names are month names, such as 7월 (July). Only the same month is replaced.Start loading CKD · GLOBAL
Hyundai · KiaFile with HMC · KMC sheets (if it has only one sheet, check Customer).Start loading Hyundai · Kia
KGMFile with AKC · project · LAW_KGM · item mapping sheets.Start loading KGM
Integrated price · CKD priceUnit price tables for the Integrated Forecast.… Start loading
If you turn off Replace that month only, the old months of shipment data · production actuals are all deleted (orange box Replace All). When you upload one month again, always keep it on.
  • To remove a weekly S/L uploaded by mistake, choose that round in Delete S/L snapshot below and click Delete.
  • Stock count: if the same count date already exists, that day's data is replaced completely. In Accumulated Count Rounds below, the ★ Latest date is the basis of the calculation.
  • Shipment · production actual files with two years mixed are saved separately by year.
  • BOM files are not uploaded here → BOM Management › Settings.

Edit the unit price tables (Price management · Integrated price · CKD price)

Step by step · Enter unit prices
  1. Click the Price Management tab.

    If some part numbers have no unit price, the yellow box N part numbers without unit price appears at the top. Click a part number chip to add it as a row, or click Add all to list to add all of them to the table at once.

  2. Click a cell in the table and correct the unit price.

    For a new row, click Add Row (the region is set to Vietnam, so change it for India part numbers).

  3. It is saved automatically after 2.5 seconds.

    To save right away, click Save Changes; to undo, click Discard. Edit N · Add N · Delete N pending is shown at the top right.

Row colors: Edited yellow · New green · Deleted red (cancel with ↺). View buttons: All · Zero Price · Changed. Integrated price is split by group (VF VN · VF India · KGM · HKMC); CKD price is split into GLOBAL · CKD · multiple prices.

Part numbers used in shipments · production · stock · plans cannot be deleted (their amounts would disappear). To leave one out of the totals only, set its unit price to 0.

Materials MES data (purchases · outsourced processing · goods sales · overseas shipments)

Step by step · Upload MES data
  1. Drag and drop the file downloaded from MES (.xls · .xlsx) onto the matching tab, without changing it.

    Purchases = supplier closing purchases (raw materials · goods), outsourced processing = supplier closing production, goods sales = goods sales data status, overseas shipments = product sales data status (overseas shipment quantity).

  2. Check the automatic check result.

    Kind · period · source N rows → saved N rows · by month. If it is a different kind: This file is 「…」 data. Please upload it in that tab.

  3. (Kind) Start loading → confirm replacement → Proceed.

    In the Loading complete window, check the replaced period · new rows · old rows deleted.

  4. In Recent upload log below, check that it shows Done.

    Materials screens that were open reload automatically.

Only data of the same kind in the period contained in the file is replaced. The old data is replaced only after all new data is added, so even if it fails partway, the old data remains. Outsourced processing is for supplier checks, so it is not subtracted from stock twice.

Admin cards

  • Main screen popup videos (up to 3): Expand → title · video file (100 MB or less) for each tab → Save. To remove one, click Remove.
  • Export performance week settings: fiscal year start month · base date · week split method → Preview → Save.
  • Export performance weekly file upload: The file is uploaded as soon as you choose it. If the period overlaps, it asks whether to Overwrite.

Account Management · Access Log

Decide who can see which screens, and see a log of who did what and when. Top tabs: Account Management / Access Log.

Create accounts · Give access

Step by step · New account
  1. Click New Account at the top right.
  2. Enter ID · password · name (required) and employee number · email · department (team) · position.

    The ID cannot be changed after creation. The email is the work email for login verification codes. If the person uses Tooling Cost ICIMS, put 'Sales' or 'Purchasing' in the department; to confirm and issue Free Issue Requests, put '생산관리' (Production Control).

  3. Choose the Role.

    User · Admin · Master. Admins · Masters see all screens.

  4. For a User, check the Accessible Screens.

    Turn on only what is needed. Select all / Clear All.

  5. Click Save.

    Date/time · who · kind · target · content · result. Failed rows have a light pink background. If there are several login failures in a row, check them.

  4. If needed, download with Download Excel (CSV).

What is recorded: logins · account changes · uploads · invoices · shipments · unit prices · settings changes (just viewing is not recorded). Choose the Retention period from 1 month · 3 months · 6 months · 1 year (limit 50,000 rows).

Next · AI Training →
28

AI Training

Sidebar › System › Settings › AI TrainingAccess: Master only

Create and refine the question-and-answer pairs that AI Smart Search answers right away (instant answers), without searching the data. Tabs: Pending approval (N) / KB Management (N).

Step by step · Turn frequent questions into instant answers
  1. Open the Pending approval tab.

    Questions asked two or more times, or not answered well, appear here (Frequency N · Failed N · score).

  2. Correct the Answer (edit, then approve) field.

    The latest AI answer is already filled in. If needed, enter Aliases (typos · abbreviations, separated by commas).

  3. Click Approve.

    It moves to the KB and is answered right away from then on. If it is not needed, click Ignore. To add it as an alias of an existing item, choose that item and click Merge.

  • KB Management › Add instant answer: question pattern · answer · aliases → Save. Each card has Edit · Disable/Enable · Delete.
  • If the first question of a conversation contains a question pattern or alias, that answer is shown right away (no tables · charts). Spacing matters, so add both forms as aliases, for example 'pick up' and 'pickup'.
Do not put numbers that change every day in an answer. They would become fixed text. Number questions such as stock · shippable quantity skip the KB and are always calculated from the actual data.
Next · Frequently asked questions →
29

Frequently asked questions

When you are stuck, look here first. Click a title to open the answer.

Login · Screens

I cannot see a menu

You have no access to that screen. Nothing is broken. Ask an admin (Master) for the screens you need. → Roles and access

I forgot my password

On the login screen, click Forgot your password? and enter your ID · name · employee number. A temporary password (valid for 30 minutes) appears. → Forgot password

The verification code email does not arrive

Check your spam folder, and after 1 minute click Resend code. If the email address in your account is wrong, ask an admin to correct it.

I was logged out while working

If you do nothing for 30 minutes, you are logged out automatically. Click Keep using in the window that appears 2 minutes before, and you can continue. The installed Android app and the VF Shipment Management screen (08:00–19:00) are exceptions.

The numbers are still old · I cannot see data uploaded by someone else

Data updates by itself, but if you are typing in an input field, it changes after you finish. If it still does not change, press F5 (refresh). In Materials, click Recalculate.

A downloaded Excel file shows a format warning

Click Yes and it opens normally. If you save it under another name, it becomes a normal Excel file.

Stock · Shipments

Stock looks lower than the real quantity

Available Stock subtracts the quantity of pending invoices that have not left yet. If the goods have already left, click Ship in VF Shipment Management. The deduction is released.

This month's target (S/L) does not change

This is normal. This month's target is fixed by the last S/L round of the previous month, so it does not change in the middle of the month. The backorder uses the latest round.

I cannot upload the same invoice again

Add a higher Rev number to the file name, such as _Rev01. A same or lower Rev is not added. → Upload invoices

An invoice is not shown in Shipment Management

Check that it was saved in INV Issue, or that the Excel file was uploaded to Available Stock. Also check that View is Waiting, and that no region · transport · search condition is set.

The alert sound does not play

Browsers block sound until you tap the screen once. Tap the screen once and try 🔊 Sound test. If it shows 🔕 Alerts off, click it to turn alerts on. → Alerts

I uploaded the wrong file

For shipment data · production actuals, keep Replace that month only turned on and upload only that month's file again. For a weekly S/L, upload it again with the same date and Overwrite, or delete it with Delete S/L snapshot.

Some part numbers show an amount of 0

These part numbers have no unit price registered. Add the part numbers from the yellow bar in Upload › Price Management.

Materials · Email · Other

I don't see the Confirm · Issue buttons in Free Issue Requests

Confirm, issue and reject are only for people whose account department contains '생산관리' (Production Control) or '생관', and masters. Others can only request and view. Excel upload is for masters only. → Confirm · issue

A material in use shows as dormant (or the opposite)

Change it yourself with the 💤 Dormant stock switch in the material detail header. When off, it is subject to out-of-stock · order alerts again. → Dormant stock · Stop after run-out

Korean text remains in the English SL email

Click 🌐 AI English translation in the email window or fix it in English yourself. If AI translation is not available, the Korean stays as it is, so read it before sending. → Send an English SL

There is a planned receipt, so why does it say 'Receipt check needed'?

A plan is not an actual receipt. If the material is normal only when the plan arrives, or the planned date has passed, check whether the goods actually arrived and click ✓ Confirm receipt. If it is already in MES, clear it with In MES.

Can I send again right away when the email result is unclear?

First check the status · recipients · time in the send log. If it shows Partly not received, you only need to check the rejected addresses. Decide whether to send again after looking at the log and any bounce emails.

The AI answer seems wrong

Check the numbers on the screen itself using the shortcut below the answer, and click 🚩 Report answer. An admin refines it in AI Training.

Next · Glossary →
30

Glossary

Words you often see on the screens.

Orders · Plans

TermMeaning
S/LShipping List. The planned quantities the customer sends, saying when and how much they want. Received anew every week.
Round (snapshot)The order on the day an S/L was uploaded, kept as one snapshot. Called by its date, like 07-21_SL.
RollingLooking ahead over many months from one round.
BackorderOverdue quantity that should have been delivered but has not been yet.
FIRM · FORECAST · PASTConfirmed · forecast orders (order plan) · past months. The material order plan is FIRM 4 months + FORECAST 6 months; the SL sent to suppliers is up to 12 months.
Adjustment ratioThe share of the S/L expected to actually sell (usually about 80%).
FYFiscal year. From April to March of the next year.

Stock · Shipments

TermMeaning
Stock count · Actual stockQuantity counted directly in the warehouse · current quantity after adding production and subtracting shipments since the count.
Available stockQuantity you can really ship (Sea: + planned production, Air: − packing pending).
ShippableAvailable stock minus pending invoices.
S/L RemainingThe part of this month's target not yet filled.
RevRevision number of an invoice corrected and issued again. Higher = newer.
Pickup Request · BlockedA promise: "please ship on this day with this truck" · a mark: "this pallet cannot be shipped".
P/L No · NET · GROSSPallet serial number · weight of the goods only · weight including packing.
Forwarder · DGTransport agent · Dangerous Goods (airbag explosives, etc.).

Materials

TermMeaning
Lead timeNumber of days from order to receipt. Can be edited with ✎ in the material detail.
Free issueGoods sent out free of charge, such as samples and take-outs. In Free Issue Requests: request → Production Control confirms → issued.
DormantA material with no usage in the last 30 days · next 60 days. You can set or undo it yourself with the 💤 switch in the detail.
Stop after run-out (discontinued after run-out)A material that uses only its remaining stock and is not ordered again. Left out of order alerts.
Planned departure · Shipment confirmedRecords of the departure date the supplier gave · the day it actually left (tracking no. · B/L).
Reorder dateExpected run-out date − lead time.
Planned receipt · Confirm receiptPlan for what will arrive · record of what actually arrived.
MESIn-house production · purchasing system. Its purchase data is the basis for actual receipts.
LedgerThe Finance & Accounting team's month-end stock ledger. The starting point of the stock calculation.
≈A value estimated, for example from an average, for a month with no data.

Product abbreviations

AbbreviationProductAbbreviationProduct
DABDriver airbagPABPassenger airbag
SABSide airbagCABCurtain airbag
KABKnee airbagCeABCenter airbag
STWSteering wheelFRT SB · RR SBFront · rear seat belt
FRT BKL · RR BKLFront · rear seat buckleOthersEverything else
